Complements regular content blockers.","support_email":null,"support_url":{"outgoing":"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/661241ce2f096bdaec2da15bcabd06d98276b2f13a8622e78fd4dbfeb1ac2d6a/https%3A//decentraleyes.org","url":"https://decentraleyes.org"},"tags":["anti tracker","content blocker","privacy","security"],"type":"extension","url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/addon/decentraleyes/","weekly_downloads":2383},{"authors":[{"id":11033762,"name":"kkapsner","url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/user/11033762/","username":"kkapsner"}],"average_daily_users":36688,"categories":["privacy-security"],"contributions_url":"","created":"2014-08-04T13:14:21Z","currentVersionId":6146768,"default_locale":"en-US","description":"This add-on allows users to prevent websites from using some Javascript APIs to fingerprint them. Users can choose to block the APIs entirely on some or all websites (which may break some websites) or fake its fingerprinting-friendly readout API.\n\nIf you encounter any problems please check the <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/baca5a132740489ab2fcd8d42c43f813c7341bdbfa055c3e62db45c1ae565e3d/https%3A//canvasblocker.kkapsner.de/faq/\" rel=\"nofollow\">FAQ</a> first. Please report issues and feature requests at <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/ad0241420c72c73e3fc8947c427bac973ed406aaa29cf43b7d86e17ab8337028/https%3A//github.com/kkapsner/CanvasBlocker/issues\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://github.com/kkapsner/CanvasBlocker/issues</a>\n\n<b>IMPORTANT</b>: you should only have ONE addon/setting set that protects an API. Otherwise you could face massive performance issues. (E.g. EclipsedMoon for Palemoon has 'canvas.poison' which is known to cause issues: <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/6472dc0aaf3dc2f72d7237d63fe2a4e5ce6c93db53a296e982816d85b53d8f83/https%3A//github.com/kkapsner/CanvasBlocker/issues/253%23issuecomment-459499290\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://github.com/kkapsner/CanvasBlocker/issues/253#issuecomment-459499290</a>)\nBut setting privacy.resistFingerprinting to true and/or using the new fingerprinting protection introduced with Firefox 67 is fine.\n\nProtected \"fingerprinting\" APIs:\n\t<ul>\t\t<li>canvas 2d</li>\t\t<li>webGL</li>\t\t<li>audio</li>\t\t<li>history</li>\t\t<li>window (disabled by default)</li>\t\t<li>DOMRect</li>\t\t<li>navigator (disabled by default)</li>\t\t<li>screen</li>\t</ul>\nMore information on fingerprinting can be found at:\n\t<ul>\t\t<li> &lt;canvas&gt;: <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/88000ff79289b7d11d399bb0182e79bb8001e9f3c3f529a92c69cf9be9dc0e70/http%3A//www.browserleaks.com/canvas\" rel=\"nofollow\">http://www.browserleaks.com/canvas</a></li>\t\t<li> audio:\n\t\t\t<ul>\t\t\t\t<li><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/d340daa744fc5b4422064afa74f19f6a2464f75a67971c236682daa8c76fdbf7/https%3A//audiofingerprint.openwpm.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://audiofingerprint.openwpm.com/</a> (very poorly written = slow)</li>\t\t\t\t<li><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/a0bf0420d30340713043c17cbbafe6387f62df1f7bcd19b5b740a9436988f306/https%3A//webtransparency.cs.princeton.edu/webcensus/%23audio-fp\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://webtransparency.cs.princeton.edu/webcensus/#audio-fp</a></li>\t\t\t</ul>\t\t</li>\t\t<li> DOMRect:\n\t\t\t<ul>\t\t\t\t<li><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/d6bf82a8be7a5bc84b387f17f0a3c43880d5cb83163cb0a4abb2be22e59c9ef1/http%3A//jcarlosnorte.com/security/2016/03/06/advanced-tor-browser-fingerprinting.html\" rel=\"nofollow\">http://jcarlosnorte.com/security/2016/03/06/advanced-tor-browser-fingerprinting.html</a></li>\t\t\t\t<li><a rel=\"nofollow\">https://browserleaks.com/rects</a></li>\t\t\t</ul>\t\t</li>\t</ul>\nThe different block modes are:\n\t<ul>\t\t<li> fake: Canvas Blocker's default setting, and my favorite! will be asked if the website should be allowed to use the protected APIs each time they are called.</li>\t\t<li> block everything: Ignore all lists and block the protected APIs on all websites.</li>\t\t<li> allow only white list: Only websites in the white list are allowed to use the protected APIs.</li>\t\t<li> block only black list: Block the protected APIs only for websites on It allows you to save:\n   - the current tab,\n   - the selected content,\n   - the selected frame,\n   - the selected link(s).\n - Select \"Annotate and save the page...\" in the context menu to:\n   - Highlight text,\n   - add notes,\n   - remove content.\n - You can also process multiple tabs in one click and save:\n   - the selected tabs,\n   - the unpinned tabs,\n   - all the tabs.\n - The context menu also allows you to activate the auto-save (after pages are being loaded) of:\n   - the current tab,\n   - the unpinned tabs,\n   - all the tabs.\n - Right-click on the SingleFile button and select \"Manage extension\" to open the options page.\n - Upload pages to Google Drive/Dropbox/GitHub/WebDAV via the Destination section in the options page.\n - Enable the option \"Misc. &gt; add proof of existence\" to prove the existence of saved pages by linking the SHA256 of the pages into the blockchain.\n - You can use the customizable shortcut Ctrl+Shift+Y to save the current tab or selected tabs.\n - And more!\n\nMore info\n - FAQ: <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/33a61f69aa3f23a1e8aa10062ba4c6ec3d22667b92ec6df6030b590095491db6/https%3A//github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile/blob/master/faq.md\" Tab Discard\" is a lightweight browser extension that automatically reduces the amount of memory used by open but inactive tabs, helping to conserve battery life on portable devices by disabling JavaScript code and DOM events on discarded tabs.\n\nPreview:\n\n<a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/6656d3746bb302371367c16e0eefcc3c09ec6026c9471acf1b271ff517c694cd/https%3A//www.youtube.com/watch%3Fv=ityhxUPp3y8\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ityhxUPp3y8</a>\n\nInstruction:\n\nThere are two options on the extension's interface to either discard tabs or release discarding (the \"x\" buttons)\n\n\nThe features of \"Auto Tab Discard\" are:\n\n  * Improves browser performance and reduces memory usage by automatically or manually discarding inactive, idle, or forgotten tabs.\n  * Includes a list of hostnames or regular expressions that can be excluded from auto-discarding.\n  * Preserves the discarded state of tabs when the browser is closed and reopened.\n  * Indicates the discarded state of a tab through the favicon or title.\n  * Restores the state of a tab, including scroll position and text box content, after it is released from being discarded.\n  * Allows for the forced discarding of specified hostnames.\n\n\nThe options for configuring whether tabs should be discarded or not include:\n\n  * Pinned tabs\n  * Tabs that are playing media\n  * The presence of a power source\n  * Unsaved content or user input in a tab\n\nThe available plugins for \"Auto Tab Discard\" are:\n\n  * Release the discarded state of the next or previous tab.\n  * Release the discarded state of all tabs in the active window.\n  * Automatically discard all newly opened inactive tabs.\n  * Store YouTube's timestamp before a tab is discarded.\n  * Permanently delete (remove) old discarded tabs that are inactive.\n\nNotes:\n\n  * Unlike the \"The Great Suspender\" extension, this extension uses the native method for discarding tabs, which means that even if the extension is removed or the browser crashes, your browser will automatically restore everything for you.\n\nPrivacy Policy:\n\n<a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/150e4012f598980a75fd7e6550f9b64a73356e189639f825a6e6cd6ce36344f7/https%3A//webextension.org/privacy-policy/extension/tab-discard.html\" Domain icons for tab URLs are generated by Google.\n\nThe only exception is if you intentionally use the “share as a web page” feature, which uploads your tab list to a web page so you can share it with others. Whether you’re looking to fine-tune playback settings or customize the look and feel, this YouTube enhancer gives you complete control to tailor YouTube to your needs.\n\nKey Features:\n\nVideo Playback:\n• Quickly adjust playback speed, quality, volume, and more with the mouse wheel or keyboard shortcuts.\n• Playback Speed: Set a default playback speed and adjust it anywhere from 0.07x to 16x while watching.\n• Playback Quality: Set a default playback quality for videos, playlists, and embedded videos.\n• Volume Booster: Boost volume and bass for louder, clearer sound (up to 10x audio boost).\n• Enhanced Loop Mode: Repeat specific parts or the entire video effortlessly.\n• Autoplay Control: Fine-tune or disable YouTube’s autoplay features for better control.\n• Video Formats: Disable high frame rate formats (60fps).\n\nAppearance and Themes:\n• Video Filters: Adjust brightness, contrast, color, and more without changing your monitor's settings.\n• Custom Themes: Personalize YouTube's appearance with custom themes to suit your style.\n• Cinema Mode: Dim everything around the player to keep your focus on the video.\n\nVideo Player:\n• Wide Player: Expand the player beyond the default Theater Mode, or fill the entire screen without going full screen.\n• Mini Player: Keep watching while reading comments so you never miss a moment.\n\nDistractions and Shorts:\n• Block Distractions: Hide end screens, info cards, recommendations, related videos, chat, or comments.\n• YouTube Shorts: Hide Shorts or watch Shorts like regular videos.\n\nOther Features:\n• Screenshot: Capture high-quality screenshots of your favorite video moments.\n• Keyboard Shortcuts: Set up customizable keyboard shortcuts to control YouTube like a pro.\n\nAnd more!\n\nGet the most popular YouTube enhancer now and transform the way you watch YouTube forever!\n\n———\n\nEnhancer for YouTube™ is a third-party extension and is not affiliated with YouTube.\nYouTube is a trademark of Google LLC. Use of this trademark is subject to Google Software","picture_url":null,"url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/user/13863253/","username":"ken_love"}],"average_daily_users":211314,"categories":["feeds-news-blogging","appearance","language-support"],"contributions_url":"","created":"2018-03-19T17:25:50Z","currentVersionId":6143069,"default_locale":"en-US","description":"Tired of sitting, tired of reading?  Click a button, jump on your bed, and have the article read aloud to you.  You can choose from many available male and female voices. You can also set the pitch of the voice and the reading speed.  Supports PDF.  Supports EPUB (via the excellent <a href=\"https://addons.mozilla.org/firefox/addon/epubreader/\" rel=\"nofollow\">EPUBReader</a> extension). \n\nRead Aloud is a Firefox extension that uses text-to-speech technology to convert webpage text to audio.  It works on a variety of websites, including news sites, blogs, fan fiction, publications, textbooks, school and class websites, online universities and course materials.\n\nRead Aloud is aimed at users who prefer to listen to content instead of reading, people with dyslexia or other learning disabilities, children learning to read, or simply to provide users with alternative way to consume web content.\n\nRead Aloud allows you to select from a variety of text-to-speech voices, including those provided natively by the browser, as well as by text-to-speech cloud service providers such as Google Wavenet, Amazon Polly, IBM Watson, and Microsoft.  Some of the cloud-based voices may require additional in-app purchase to enable.\n\nRecent developments in machine learning and artificial intelligence has enabled very high-quality speech synthesis.  Google Wavenet, Amazon Polly voices, for example, use bleeding edge technology to provide life-like synthetic speech that is close to that of human speech.  Read Aloud allows you to use your own Google Cloud Platform and Amazon Web Services account to enable these voices.\n\nTo use Read Aloud, simply navigate to the web page you want to read, then click the Read Aloud icon on the Firefox menu.  In addition, the shortcut keys ALT-P, ALT-O, ALT-Comma, and ALT-Period can be used to Play/Pause, Stop, Rewind, and Forward, respectively.  You may select the text you want to read before activating the extension.  Right clicking on the selected text will provide you with yet another context-menu option to activate Read Aloud.\n\nTo change the voice, reading speed, pitch, or enable text highlighting, go to the Options page either by right clicking on the Read Aloud icon and choose Options, or by clicking the Gear button on the extension popup (you'll need to stop playback to see the Gear button).\n\n<strong>Privacy Policy</strong>\n\n<i>Personal information</i>\nIf you use one of the Premium Voices, you'll be asked to sign in to your Google account and grant Read Aloud access to your basic user profile, which includes name and email address. We require this information for usage tracking, since the Premium Voices requires making a purchase when usage exceeds the monthly free tier. If you don't use the Premium Voices, you don't need to sign in, and no information is collected.\n\n<i>Browsing history</i>\nWe do not track the user's browsing history. We realize the importance of privacy, so we make sure our extensions require only the minimum set of browser permissions to operate.  In particular, during installation, it does not ask for the \"Read your browsing history\" or the \"Read and change all your data on the websites you visit\" permissions, both of which could be used to track the user's browsing history.\n\n<i>Text converted to speech</i>\nWhen you select one of the standard text-to-speech voices, the speech synthesis occurs locally on your computer and is never sent to the internet. And neither the input text nor the synthesized speech are saved or stored on your computer.\n\nWhen you select one of the premium text-to-speech voices, the input text is sent to our cloud server for synthesis. The server may use your anonymized IP address and User-Agent data for purpose of analytics, abuse management, and service improvement. The server may cache the input text, as well as the synthesized speech, for short amounts of time as part of its normal operation; it does not save them to any type of permanent storage.\n\n<i>Geolocation</i>\nIf you select a voice that is synthesized on our server, your anonymized IP address may be used for geolocation for analytics purposes.\n\n<i>Issue reporting and contact forms</i>\nIf you use the in-app report-issue function or use the contact form on our support website, your comments and information may be kept in our support database for a maximum of 6 months.\n\n<i>Analytics</i>\nThe extensions use Firefox Addons Store's built-in analytics. Firefox will collect basic extension usage data, including when the extension is installed, invoked, and uninstalled. The extension may also use its own counters to analyze usage patterns. This information is anonymized and do not present privacy concerns.","developer_comments":"To report an issue, please use the \"report issue\" feature inside the extension.  You can also use Support link above.This is an open-source project. Click a button, jump on your bed, and have the article read aloud to you.  You can choose from many available male and female voices. You can also set the pitch of the voice and the reading speed.  Supports PDF.  Supports EPUB (via the excellent <a href=\"https://addons.mozilla.org/firefox/addon/epubreader/\" rel=\"nofollow\">EPUBReader</a> extension). \n\nRead Aloud is a Firefox extension that uses text-to-speech technology to convert webpage text to audio.  It works on a variety of websites, including news sites, blogs, fan fiction, publications, textbooks, school and class websites, online universities and course materials.\n\nRead Aloud is aimed at users who prefer to listen to content instead of reading, people with dyslexia or other learning disabilities, children learning to read, or simply to provide users with alternative way to consume web content.\n\nRead Aloud allows you to select from a variety of text-to-speech voices, including those provided natively by the browser, as well as by text-to-speech cloud service providers such as Google Wavenet, Amazon Polly, IBM Watson, and Microsoft.  Some of the cloud-based voices may require additional in-app purchase to enable.\n\nRecent developments in machine learning and artificial intelligence has enabled very high-quality speech synthesis.  Google Wavenet, Amazon Polly voices, for example, use bleeding edge technology to provide life-like synthetic speech that is close to that of human speech.  Read Aloud allows you to use your own Google Cloud Platform and Amazon Web Services account to enable these voices.\n\nTo use Read Aloud, simply navigate to the web page you want to read, then click the Read Aloud icon on the Firefox menu.  In addition, the shortcut keys ALT-P, ALT-O, ALT-Comma, and ALT-Period can be used to Play/Pause, Stop, Rewind, and Forward, respectively.  You may select the text you want to read before activating the extension.  Right clicking on the selected text will provide you with yet another context-menu option to activate Read Aloud.\n\nTo change the voice, reading speed, pitch, or enable text highlighting, go to the Options page either by right clicking on the Read Aloud icon and choose Options, or by clicking the Gear button on the extension popup (you'll need to stop playback to see the Gear button).\n\n<strong>Privacy Policy</strong>\n\n<i>Personal information</i>\nIf you use one of the Premium Voices, you'll be asked to sign in to your Google account and grant Read Aloud access to your basic user profile, which includes name and email address. We require this information for usage tracking, since the Premium Voices requires making a purchase when usage exceeds the monthly free tier. If you don't use the Premium Voices, you don't need to sign in, and no information is collected.\n\n<i>Browsing history</i>\nWe do not track the user's browsing history. We realize the importance of privacy, so we make sure our extensions require only the minimum set of browser permissions to operate.  In particular, during installation, it does not ask for the \"Read your browsing history\" or the \"Read and change all your data on the websites you visit\" permissions, both of which could be used to track the user's browsing history.\n\n<i>Text converted to speech</i>\nWhen you select one of the standard text-to-speech voices, the speech synthesis occurs locally on your computer and is never sent to the internet. And neither the input text nor the synthesized speech are saved or stored on your computer.\n\nWhen you select one of the premium text-to-speech voices, the input text is sent to our cloud server for synthesis. The server may use your anonymized IP address and User-Agent data for purpose of analytics, abuse management, and service improvement. The server may cache the input text, as well as the synthesized speech, for short amounts of time as part of its normal operation; it does not save them to any type of permanent storage.\n\n<i>Geolocation</i>\nIf you select a voice that is synthesized on our server, your anonymized IP address may be used for geolocation for analytics purposes.\n\n<i>Issue reporting and contact forms</i>\nIf you use the in-app report-issue function or use the contact form on our support website, your comments and information may be kept in our support database for a maximum of 6 months.\n\n<i>Analytics</i>\nThe extensions use Firefox Addons Store's built-in analytics. Firefox will collect basic extension usage data, including when the extension is installed, invoked, and uninstalled. The extension may also use its own counters to analyze usage patterns. This information is anonymized and do not present privacy concerns.","developer_comments":"To report an issue, please use the \"report issue\" feature inside the extension.  You can also use Support link above.This is an open-source project. To contribute, please visit: <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/52ea70faaa98ba3fbea8b9da1ee3e90436aa4fb671923e38cd466fb8c670a465/https%3A//github.com/ken107/read-aloud\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://github.com/ken107/read-aloud</a>","edit_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/developers/addon/read-aloud/edit","guid":"{ddc62400-f22d-4dd3-8b4a-05837de53c2e}","has_eula":false,"has_privacy_policy":true,"homepage":null,"icon_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/952/952959-64.png?modified=cccc5f2c","icons":{"128":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/952/952959-128.png?modified=cccc5f2c","32":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/952/952959-32.png?modified=cccc5f2c","64":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/952/952959-64.png?modified=cccc5f2c"},"id":952959,"isAndroidCompatible":true,"isMozillaSignedExtension":false,"is_disabled":false,"is_experimental":false,"is_noindexed":false,"last_updated":"2026-02-20T15:48:18Z","name":"Read Aloud: A Text to Speech Voice Reader","previews":[{"h":540,"src":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/previews/full/199/199553.png?modified=1622133040","thumbnail_h":400,"thumbnail_src":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/previews/thumbs/199/199553.jpg?modified=1622133040","thumbnail_w":533,"title":null,"w":720}],"promoted":[{"apps":["firefox","android"],"category":"recommended"}],"ratings":{"average":3.8129,"bayesian_average":3.808801121443065,"count":1272,"text_count":615},"requires_payment":false,"review_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/reviewers/review/952959","slug":"read-aloud","status":"public","summary":"Read out loud the current web-page article with one click. Supports 40+ languages.","support_email":"support@lsdsoftware.com","support_url":{"outgoing":"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/c77af5aefb918598cc2b42ce69364e0176d4518437816b3aba2a63d2f551486a/https%3A//readaloud.app","url":"https://readaloud.app"},"tags":[],"type":"extension","url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/addon/read-aloud/","weekly_downloads":3948},{"authors":[{"id":16148078,"name":"Dark Mode","picture_url":null,"url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/user/16148078/","username":"anonymous-828945547a0ca3fe7b9b93a6217d90d0"}],"average_daily_users":46964,"categories":["appearance"],"contributions_url":"","created":"2020-07-17T16:36:39Z","currentVersionId":5066240,"default_locale":"en-US","description":"Dark Mode extension will put dark theme on all websites, suitable for those work at night and dark reader for online news or book reading.\n\n★ What will you get from Dark Mode extension:\n1) An easy way to create dark mode on every website. In addition, you can customize your dark theme visual from Brightness, Contrast, to Saturation.\n2) A night shift mode which changes the website's color to warmer, making it much more pleasing to watch.\n3) You can set up schedule for each dark theme and night shift mode, and decide when you want to auto-activate dark theme.","developer_comments":null,"edit_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/developers/addon/dark-mode-by-albert-inc/edit","guid":"{830f38bd-efc5-45dc-a5a6-064d9a638806}","has_eula":false,"has_privacy_policy":false,"homepage":null,"icon_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2653/2653216-64.png?modified=3253ba76","icons":{"128":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2653/2653216-128.png?modified=3253ba76","32":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2653/2653216-32.png?modified=3253ba76","64":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2653/2653216-64.png?modified=3253ba76"},"id":2653216,"isAndroidCompatible":false,"isMozillaSignedExtension":false,"is_disabled":false,"is_experimental":false,"is_noindexed":false,"last_updated":"2020-07-17T18:15:11Z","name":"Dark Mode","previews":[{"h":754,"src":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/previews/full/241/241813.png?modified=1622135812","thumbnail_h":300,"thumbnail_src":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/previews/thumbs/241/241813.jpg?modified=1622135812","thumbnail_w":533,"title":null,"w":1339}],"promoted":[{"apps":["firefox"],"category":"spotlight"}],"ratings":{"average":3.1898,"bayesian_average":3.1708814562129786,"count":216,"text_count":101},"requires_payment":false,"review_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/reviewers/review/2653216","slug":"dark-mode-by-albert-inc","status":"public","summary":"Dark theme and night shift mode for every website. Care your eyes, use Dark Mode for night and daily web browsing.","support_email":null,"support_url":null,"tags":[],"type":"extension","url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/addon/dark-mode-by-albert-inc/","weekly_downloads":2015},{"authors":[{"id":13304480,"name":"mbnuqw","picture_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/userpics/80/4480/13304480/13304480.png?modified=1701083646","url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/firefox/user/13304480/","username":"mbnuqw"}],"average_daily_users":51102,"categories":["bookmarks","tabs"],"contributions_url":"","created":"2018-09-23T11:13:46Z","currentVersionId":6359518,"default_locale":"en-US","description":"Sidebery provides the list of tabs structured in a tree, bookmarks and history within the customizable panels. It aims to be fast and configurable.\n\n<strong>Notice: </strong>Sidebery may conflict with addons that handle position of new tabs (e.g. Tree Style Tabs) or addons that move/sort tabs, which may result in unexpected behavior or broken tabs state at initialization. To avoid potential issues, please, disable such addons in Add-ons Manager page (about:addons).\n\n<strong>Some of the key features:</strong>\n\n<ul><strong>- Tabs</strong><ul>- Simple flat list or tree with the option to set tree limit.\n- Group tabs (aka folders) with a custom name helps organize open pages.\n- Tabs colorization: Automatic per-domain/URL or manual.\n- Tabs sorting: by title, url or access time\n- Automatic unloading of tabs in a folded branch / inactive panel.\n- Customizable tab title.\n- ...</ul><strong>- Bookmarks</strong><ul>- Tree or chronological view.\n- Sort by name, URL or creation time\n- Basic operations: open in... / create / edit / delete.\n- Ability to highlight open bookmarks and activate the corresponding tab instead of opening a new one when clicked.\n- Automatically delete a bookmark on opening from \"Other Bookmarks\" folder or configured bookmarks panel.\n- ...</ul><strong>- Tabs / Bookmarks panels</strong><ul>- Organize your browsing with panels of tabs/bookmarks.\n- Convert open tabs in the panel to bookmarks and vice versa.\n- Automatically move tabs by URL to configured panel.\n- ...</ul><strong>- Containers</strong><ul>- Bind container to the tabs panel.\n- Automatically reopen tab in the target container by URL.\n- Setup proxy config and UserAgent header for tabs in a container.\n- ...</ul><strong>- Customizable context menu</strong><ul>Sidebery allows you to change the context menu for tabs and bookmarks. You can enable/disable/move options, create sub-menus/separators.</ul><strong>- Customizable mouse/keyboard controls</strong><ul>- Option to switch tabs with mouse-wheel.\n- Configurable drag and drop behavior.\n- A growing list of available actions for mouse/keyboard bindings.</ul><strong>- Customizable styles</strong><ul>Sidebery provides full control of styles for sidebar and group page via variables and custom CSS.</ul><strong>- Snapshots</strong><ul>You can setup auto snapshots that will keep info about open windows and tabs.</ul></ul>\n\n<strong>Privacy policy</strong>\n<ul>- Sidebery does not engage in ad targeting, data collection, or other activities that could compromise your privacy.\n- By default, all data is stored locally on your machine.\n- Sidebery sends data only when synchronization is enabled and only to chosen provider.\n- You can optionally enable one or more synchronization providers (third-party services) to sync your data across multiple devices.\nMore detailed information can be found on this page: <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/c811ae0071e07243a92f0ec682304de80cdab07aaa77915e2248ed17b3f5f66c/https%3A//github.com/mbnuqw/sidebery/blob/v5/PRIVACY.md\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://github.com/mbnuqw/sidebery/blob/v5/PRIVACY.md</a></ul>\n\n<strong>Source code</strong>\n<ul>Source code can be found here: <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/176b20d58a82cb3204b15a8abdeabcc5758cc92b19ae704e19e4befe9726bbf3/https%3A//github.com/mbnuqw/sidebery\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://github.com/mbnuqw/sidebery</a></ul>\n\n<strong>Permissions</strong>\n<ul>- \"Access recently closed tabs\" - needed for the \"Undo close tab\" function.\n- \"Access browser tabs\" - needed to get info and control tabs.\n- \"Store unlimited amount of client-side data\" - needed to store favicons cache and snapshots.</ul>\n\n<strong>Optional permissions</strong>\n<ul>- \"Access your data for all websites\" and \"Control browser proxy settings\"\n<ul>Required for the next features:\n- Context menu option for cleaning cookies\n- Proxy and url rules of containers\n- Page-screenshots for the group page and windows selection panel\n- Changing the User-Agent per container</ul>- \"Hide and show browser tabs\"<ul>Required for the next features:\n- Hiding tabs on inactive panels\n- Hiding folded tabs</ul>- \"Read and modify bookmarks\" - needed for bookmarking the tabs and bookmarks panel functionality.\n- \"Access browsing history\" - for history panel functionality.\n- \"Input data to the clipboard\" - Context menu option for copying urls of tabs and bookmarks\n- \"Download files and read and modify the browser’s download history\" - Auto-export of snapshots.\n\nNote: You can deny these permissions at any time on the settings page.</ul>\n<strong>Notes</strong>\n<ul>- Firefox doesn't allow addons to hide the native (horizontal) tab bar. To achieve that, you can use <a can the <abbr title=\"TWP - Translate Web Pages\">TWP</abbr> extension do?</strong>\n<ul><li>Your page is translated in one click without opening new tabs.</li><li>Translate PDF documents.</li><li>Translate selected text.</li><li>Multiple translation services are supported.</li></ul>\n<strong>Why do you need to access your data on all the websites you visit?</strong>\nThis permission is required to translate the websites.  \n\n<strong>How are the pages translated?</strong>\nPages are translated in real time using the translation service of your choice.  \n\n<strong>And how's my privacy?</strong>\n<a href=\"https://addons.mozilla.org/addon/traduzir-paginas-web/privacy/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Privacy policy</a> We do not collect any data. However, the translation is done using third-party services and they may collect data to improve their services.  \n\n<strong>Limitations</strong>\nSome pages like <em><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/010dd65101055af98afea99c68dceb6418c1e21a093b0bfae00d21f8c5ac1171/http%3A//support.mozilla.org\" rel=\"nofollow\">support.mozilla.org</a></em> and <em><a href=\"http://addons.mozilla.org\" rel=\"nofollow\">addons.mozilla.org</a></em> will not be translated. For security reasons, the browser blocks extensions from accessing these sites.  \n\n<strong>How can I help you?</strong>\n<ul><li><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/3e74314ba42488c3bf933a06bd03d6e26ec079d17ded544e729d1423a020d553/https%3A//www.patreon.com/filipeps\" rel=\"nofollow\">Patreon</a> To help this project financially.</li><li><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/858c81d3783ce4d0de621cbbf6d55423d93175dea9f831a38a258304c7475812/https%3A//github.com/FilipePS/Traduzir-paginas-web\" rel=\"nofollow\">GitHub</a> To report bugs and contribute to the extension's source code.</li><li><a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/783d76b54e88f07d32567c10a7a29657d2b9b7e4482ecd74fcc13628026b15ae/https%3A//crowdin.com/project/translate-web-pages\" rel=\"nofollow\">Crowdin</a> For interface localization.</li></ul>","developer_comments":null,"edit_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/en-US/developers/addon/traduzir-paginas-web/edit","guid":"{036a55b4-5e72-4d05-a06c-cba2dfcc134a}","has_eula":false,"has_privacy_policy":true,"homepage":null,"icon_url":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2623/2623538-64.png?modified=8be60eed","icons":{"128":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2623/2623538-128.png?modified=8be60eed","32":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2623/2623538-32.png?modified=8be60eed","64":"https://addons.mozilla.org/user-media/addon_icons/2623/2623538-64.png?modified=8be60eed"},"id":2623538,"isAndroidCompatible":true,"isMozillaSignedExtension":false,"is_disabled":false,"is_experimental":false,"is_noindexed":null,"last_updated":"2025-03-28T09:31:22Z","name":"TWP - If you'd like to support the continued development of the extension, please consider contributing with <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/d30d7db2a11abf1763ee268e0dc09b4f3ca69c2f961d352286c8deac86b31a90/https%3A//go.vapps.dev/patreon%3Fpr=search-by-image&amp;src=amo\" rel=\"nofollow\">Patreon</a>, <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/88ba58cceba1789cd7f9c58528c611770f4f877d40721a45bfd5e4920947eacc/https%3A//go.vapps.dev/paypal%3Fpr=search-by-image&amp;src=amo\" rel=\"nofollow\">PayPal</a> or <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/5079314881b28aea594cf25e4a1f988d56befd83ec619c43e6eb2214d4747e6f/https%3A//go.vapps.dev/bitcoin%3Fpr=search-by-image&amp;src=amo\" rel=\"nofollow\">Bitcoin</a>.\n\nThe extension helps journalists and researchers verify the authenticity of images, and assists in the identification of false information on social media. Search by Image is also popular among photographers, helping artists explore how their work is shared on the web, while shoppers find it valuable for discovering similar products at discounted prices.\n\nThe extension enables you to search for images from the context menu and the browser toolbar with the help of a diverse set of reverse image search engines, which can be toggled and reordered from the extension's options. Visit the wiki for the full list of supported search engines.\n\n<a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/a1f33afb4059aef86430eb9249e5a6024756138e53dc9117ae49eaf455990b69/https%3A//github.com/dessant/search-by-image/wiki/Search-engines\" rel=\"nofollow\">https://github.com/dessant/search-by-image/wiki/Search-engines</a>\n\nImages positioned at the selected area are detected regardless of how they were embedded in the page. The extension also supports searching with images from your device, images from private sites, and captured areas of the page.\n\nA handful of search modes are offered that serve different use cases. The search mode can be set independently for the context menu and the browser toolbar from the extension's options.\n\n<strong>Search modes:</strong>\n<ul><li><strong>Select URL</strong>: select an image on the page and search with the image URL. This is the default search mode.</li><li><strong>Select image</strong>: select an image on the page and search with the image file. This search mode is preferred for sites which do not allow the direct linking of images, such as private sites.</li><li><strong>Capture</strong>: search with a captured area of the page.</li><li><strong>Browse</strong>: search with an image from your device. Pasting images from the clipboard is also supported on desktop platforms.</li><li><strong>URL</strong>: search with an image URL.</li></ul>\nKnown issues:\n<ul><li>Firefox Multi-Account Containers replaces created tabs and breaks the extension (<a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/fe0cfbf64c3407f7a5d07375f1f9d947c30596f393068addae439a7c2f848585/https%3A//github.com/dessant/search-by-image/issues/371\" rel=\"nofollow\">issue</a>)</li></ul>\nThe extension is also available for <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/7ed09b9ef47c6ca28528b04a7fc4f9d47d06cbe14963b3b2078b69c752144ac6/https%3A//chrome.google.com/webstore/detail/search-by-image/cnojnbdhbhnkbcieeekonklommdnndci\" rel=\"nofollow\">Chrome</a> and <a href=\"https://prod.outgoing.prod.webservices.mozgcp.net/v1/32275a8494767c60b0f8570f5f7abcf126ad0a6351f4a5deccac0f396007ac46/https%3A//apps.apple.com/us/app/search-by-image-for-safari/id1544552106\" rel=\"nofollow\">Safari</a>.\n\nThe image you select during a search is sent to the requested service, visit the extension's <a href=\"https://addons.mozilla.org/firefox/addon/search_by_image/privacy/\" rel=\"nofollow\">privacy policy</a> for more information.\n\n<strong>Reviews are not monitored for bug reports, please use
